[S]Evolution is the change in the inherited characteristics of biological populations over successive generations.[E]
[S]The concept of entropy relates to the level of disorder or randomness in a system.[E]
[S]The carbon cycle is the flow of carbon through the atmosphere, oceans, and land.[E]
[S]Climate change is the significant and lasting change in global weather patterns.[E]
[S]Biodiversity refers to the variety of life on Earth.[E]
[S]Sustainability aims to meet the needs of the present without compromising future generations' ability to meet their own needs.[E]
[S]A renewable resource can be naturally replenished such as wind and solar power.[E]
[S]A non-renewable resource is finite, like fossil fuels.[E]
[S]Globalization is the increasing interconnectedness of countries through trade, travel, and communication.[E]
[S]Urbanization is the growth of cities.[E]
[S]Migration is the movement of people from one place to another.[E]
[S]Artificial intelligence (AI) is the simulation of human intelligence in machines.[E]
[S]Machine learning allows computers to learn from data without explicit programming.[E]
[S]Data science is a field that extracts knowledge and insights from data.[E]
[S]The blockchain is a technology for creating secure and transparent records of transactions.[E]
[S]Cryptocurrency is a digital or virtual currency secured by cryptography.[E]
[S]Cybersecurity is the practice of protecting computer systems and networks from digital attacks.[E]
[S]Genetics studies the inheritance and variation of trait's in organisms.[E]
[S]The study of Astronomy is based on celestial objects, space, and the universe.[E]
[S]Medicine is the branch of health care focused on prevention and treatment of illnesses.[E]
[S]Engineering uses scientific and mathematical principles to design and build structures and systems.[E]
[S]Architecture is the design of buildings and other physical structures.[E]
[S]Agriculture is the practice of farming to produce food and other products.[E]
[S]The legal system includes rules, laws, and judicial processes.[E]
[S]Bureaucracy refers to the administrative system in government or large organization.[E]
[S]Diplomacy is the management of international relations.[E]
[S]Nationalism is a strong identification with a nation and it's culture.[E]
[S]Patriotism is love for one's country.[E]
[S]A constitution is a basic law of the land or state.[E]
[S]A treaty is a formal agreement between countries or states.[E]
[S]The concept of human rights refers to basic freedoms and rights all people should have.[E]

[S]The sky is often a gradient of blue, becoming lighter towards the horizon.[E]
[S]Grass is typically green due to the presence of chlorophyll.[E]
[S]Sunlight contains all colors of the rainbow, visible when it passes through a prism or creates a rainbow in rain.[E]
[S]Water reflects light, often appearing blue in large bodies like oceans and lakes.[E]
[S]The Earth rotates on it's axis, causing day and night.[E]
[S]The sun rises in the east and sets in the west.[E]
[S]Trees produce oxygen, which is essential for human breathing.[E]
[S]Bees pollinate flowers, enabling the growth of fruit's and vegetables.[E]
[S]Mountains are large landforms, rising above the surrounding terrain.[E]
[S]A desert is an area that receives very little rainfall.[E]
[S]The moon reflects sunlight, illuminating the night sky.[E]
[S]A compass points north, which helps with navigation.[E]
[S]The planet Earth is primarily covered by water.[E]
[S]The continents are large landmasses separated by oceans.[E]
[S]Gravity pulls objects towards the Earth's center.[E]
[S]The human body has five main senses: sight, hearing, smell, taste, and touch.[E]
[S]Birds often migrate to warmer climates in the winter months.[E]
[S]Volcanoes are openings in the Earth's crust that release molten rock and gas.[E]
[S]A rainforest is a dense forest with high rainfall and biodiversity.[E]
[S]A river is a flowing body of water, often emptying into an ocean or lake.[E]
[S]Sand is made from tiny particles of weathered rocks.[E]
[S]Snow is frozen precipitation that falls as white flakes.[E]
[S]Ice is the solid form of water, typically appearing clear or whitish.[E]
[S]The wind is caused by differences in air pressure.[E]
[S]Fog is a cloud that forms near the ground.[E]
[S]A rainbow appears when sunlight is refracted and reflected by water droplets in the air.[E]
[S]The colors of a rainbow always appear in the same order: red, orange, yellow, green, blue, indigo, and violet.[E]
[S]A clock measures time using hours, minutes, and seconds.[E]
[S]A calendar tracks the days, weeks, and months of a year.[E]
[S]Different cultures have their own unique traditions and customs.[E]
[S]A thermometer measures the temperature of things, often in degrees Celsius or Fahrenheit.[E]
[S]A barometer measures atmospheric pressure, which is related to the weather.[E]
[S]An animal breathes, typically using lungs or gills.[E]
[S]A plant gets it's nutrients from the soil and sun using the process of photosynthesis.[E]
[S]A circle is a two-dimensional shape where every point on the edge is the same distance from it's center.[E]
[S]A square is a four-sided shape with all sides equal in length and all angles at 90 degrees.[E]
[S]A triangle is a three-sided shape.[E]
[S]An oval is a closed curve resembling an egg.[E]
[S]A map is a representation of a particular area, often to scale.[E]
[S]The equator is an imaginary line dividing the Earth into the Northern and Southern hemispheres.[E]
[S]The North and South poles are the furthest points of the Earth from the equator.[E]
[S]A hurricane is a powerful tropical storm with strong winds and heavy rainfall.[E]
[S]A tornado is a violently rotating column of air touching both the ground and a cloud.[E]
[S]An earthquake is a sudden shaking of the ground due to tectonic movement.[E]
[S]Light travels very fast in a straight line and can be refracted by a lens.[E]
[S]Sound travels in waves through the air or a medium.[E]
[S]Different types of energy include kinetic, potential, thermal, and electrical.[E]
[S]A magnet is an object that attracts or repels other objects, usually made of metal.[E]
[S]Molecules are formed by two or more atoms bonded together.[E]
[S]Chemical reactions involve the rearrangement of atoms and molecules.[E]
[S]All matter has mass and occupies space.[E]
[S]Newton's laws of motion describe how forces affect the movement of objects.[E]
[S]The speed of light is a universal constant and is approximately 300,000 kilometers per second.[E]
[S]Photosynthesis is the process where plants convert light energy into chemical energy using water and carbon dioxide.[E]
[S]Respiration is the process by which organisms convert food into energy, often using oxygen.[E]
[S]Evolution is the process of gradual change in organisms over many generations.[E]
[S]DNA is the molecule that carries genetic information and that dictates trait's.[E]
[S]Genes are specific sequences of DNA that influence inherited trait's.[E]
[S]Cell is the basic structural and functional unit of life.[E]
[S]Organism is any individual form of life like plant, animal, bacteria or single cell.[E]
[S]Ecology studies the interactions between organisms and their environment.[E]
[S]A fossil is the preserved remains or traces of ancient life.[E]
[S]Climate is the long-term weather pattern in a specific region.[E]
[S]Weather is the state of the atmosphere at a particular place and time, encompassing things such as rain, wind, and temperature.[E]
[S]A tide is the regular rising and falling of the sea level, mostly caused by the gravitational pull of the moon and sun on Earth.[E]
[S]An asteroid is a small, rocky object orbiting the sun, usually between Mars and Jupiter.[E]
[S]A comet is an icy body that releases gas and dust, creating a tail as it approaches the sun.[E]
[S]A galaxy is a large collection of stars, gas, dust, and dark matter bound together by gravity, our own one being the Milky Way.[E]
[S]Stars are massive, luminous spheres of plasma held together by their own gravity, constantly generating and releasing large amounts of energy.[E]
[S]Planets are celestial bodies that orbit a star.[E]
[S]The universe encompasses everything that exists: space, time, energy, and matter, it contains all galaxies, stars, planets, and other celestial bodies.[E]
[S]Constellations are patterns of stars that form recognizable figures in the night sky.[E]
[S]The concept of time involves past, present, and future.[E]
[S]Every living creature and every structure has at least three dimensions: Length, Width and Height.[E]
[S]Light is a form of electromagnetic radiation and comes in varying wavelengths that correspond to different colors in the visible spectrum.[E]
[S]A shadow is a dark area formed by something blocking light.[E]
[S]A vacuum is an absence of matter and in outer space you have that condition to it's absolute limit.[E]
[S]A proton, a neutron and an electron form the basic constituents of the very thing we know as matter.[E]
[S]The mass of a proton is roughly the same as a neutron, which is more than 1800 times the mass of an electron.[E]
[S]Electrons orbit the nucleus of atoms, whereas the nucleus holds together the protons and neutrons in it's structure.[E]
[S]The pH scale measure acidity and alkalinity where zero is super acidic and 14 is super alkaline, where 7 is considered neutral like water.[E]
[S]A liquid has a constant volume but can take on the shape of the vessel it's inside, it is always moving at a microscopic level even when stationary.[E]
[S]A solid has a fixed shape and volume, but some solids can deform at very large stress values, whilst others may simply fracture instead.[E]
[S]A gas has no fixed volume nor fixed shape, and can expand and contract, filling any vessel completely and will always flow.[E]
[S]Dark matter is a mysterious substance that cannot be seen, yet contributes the large majority of the gravitational mass of the Universe, more so than matter that we can measure using traditional methods.[E]
[S]The universe is still rapidly expanding in size, from it's beginning to it's current state.[E]
